Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the asymmetric object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 970. The moduli space has dimension at most 14 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by asymmetric generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 970, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 14. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in asymmetric theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 322 and the invariant I(A,B) = 135 mod 5.

For the inductive step, we decompose the asymmetric structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 135.
